// EEL division by zero returns zero; RNBO arithmetic does not.
function eelDivide(a,b) { return b == 0 ? 0 : a/b; }
function fn__upsli() {

let tmp22 = 0;
if ((slider12 == (0 - 36))) {

} else {

function fn_schp_eqini(arg_shape, arg_f, arg_ga, arg_w) {
s_schp_gdb = arg_ga;

let tmp34 = ((s_schp_w1 - floor(tmp33)) - .5);
arg_f *= (1 + ((.3 * (1 - eelDivide((s_schp_w1 - 1),5))) * pow((1 - (abs(tmp34) * 2)),.5)));
tmp38 = arg_f;
} else {
let tmp37 = 0;
if (arg_shape == 0) {
let tmp35 = s_schp_w1;
let tmp36 = ((s_schp_w1 - floor(tmp35)) - .5);
arg_f /= (1 + ((.2 - (.15 * pow(eelDivide(s_schp_w1,5),.5))) * pow((1 - (abs(tmp36) * 2)),.2)));
arg_f *= (1.4 - (.6 * pow(eelDivide((s_schp_w1 - 1),5),.35)));
tmp37 = arg_f;
} else {
tmp37 = 0;
}
tmp38 = tmp37;
}
s_schp_Q = .7;

let tmp41 = 0;
if (arg_shape == 0) {
s_schp_b0 = eelDivide((1 + s_schp_cosf),2);
s_schp_b1 = (0 - (1 + s_schp_cosf));
s_schp_b2 = eelDivide((1 + s_schp_cosf),2);

s_schp_a2 = (1 - s_schp_alpha);

let tmp40 = 0;

s_schp_a2 = (1 - s_schp_alpha);

} else {
tmp40 = 0;
}
tmp41 = tmp40;
}

} else {
let tmp77 = 0;
if (arg_shape == 2) {
let tmp42 = 40;
let tmp43 = (0 - 40);
let tmp44 = arg_ga;
let tmp45 = maximum(tmp43, tmp44);
arg_ga = minimum(tmp42, tmp45);
let tmp46 = 0;
let tmp47: Int = trunc(s_buff+(s_nei));

} else {
